### Key Lessons
- **XLP GTC wrapper limitation was a KNOWN gap that became a hard reality this week**. Wednesday 6/24 SEC filing note flagged "Alpaca creation-date GTC preserved through natural ratchets — expiry only resets on `replace` or new order"; Tue 7/28 midday snapshot escalated to "Manual renewal required Wed or Thu pre-market"; Wed 7/29 EOD reinforced "renewal MANDATORY Thu"; Thu 7/30 EOD said "Path-A likely blocked, Path-B primary Fri"; Fri 7/31 midday hit the Path-B wrapper wall and executed Path-C. **The gap was documented, escalated, and delivered a clean fallback — but the underlying wrapper needs a PATCH endpoint for future renewals**. Add `scripts/alpaca.sh replace ORDER_ID '<json>'` as PRIORITY-1 Week 15 backlog.
- **Defensive posture (XLI/XLB/XLP three-sleeve mix) IS structurally validated under hawkish stress** (Wed +42 bps relative-green on -1.35% SPY hawkish-FOMC), but IS structurally exposed to risk-on rotation post-stress (Thu -258 bps under +1.54% SPY, Fri -95 bps under +0.55% SPY). The bot preserves capital in RISK-OFF regimes and gives back capital in RISK-ON regimes. Over long enough windows, the two effects should net toward beating SPY if the mix is calibrated for average vol; but week-to-week the relative print will oscillate strongly with regime. **Two consecutive weekly beats (Wks 12-13) followed by one weekly red (Wk 14) = pattern-consistent with defensive-cyclical strategy calibrated for slightly-below-median vol**.
- **The 6-gate XLK re-entry check has now blocked deploys 14 consecutive gate-tracked days across Weeks 12-13-14**. VIX side of gate has cleared multiple times (VIX <17 Days 1-3 Wk 13 held) but tape/price/breadth/leadership/next-catalyst-de-risked gates have been the persistent block. **VIX collapsed to 17.09 by Fri EOD post-AMZN blowout = fresh clean check available Mon 8/3**. Week 15 Day 1 = Day 15; if gate fails again, the gate calibration itself needs an explicit re-look. Two more full-clear-blocked days Mon/Tue = escalate gate-recalibration discussion.
- **Under-deployment cost RE-EXPANDED after two-week compression**. Phase gap to SPY: Wk 11 EOD -3.13% → Wk 12 EOD -2.75% (+38 bps beat) → Wk 13 EOD -1.89% (+118 bps beat) → **Wk 14 EOD ~-3.19% (-130 bps drop)**. Two weeks of steady progress erased in one FOMC-plus-risk-on-rotation week. The bot's three-sleeve mix cannot sustain SPY-beat through risk-on rotation weeks at 60% deployment; either (a) deploy the 4th sleeve when gate clears (XLK or alternate), or (b) accept the oscillating relative print as the structural cost of the defensive-cyclical mix at this concentration. **Deploy vs accept is a strategy question, not a mechanics question** — the 3-year window will answer it.
- **Push cadence 13/15 (86.7%) is a legitimate mechanics regression**, not noise. Two midday commits missing (Mon + Wed) breaks the two-week perfect 30/30 streak. Wed midday was particularly costly (FOMC day = highest-stakes single-session of the quarter, midday scan would have documented pre-FOMC positioning + XLK re-entry Day 12 scorecard). Mon midday miss is unexplained. **Investigate midday-commit gap Week 15 Mon /pre-market**: was the routine skipped intentionally (no action to take), or did the commit-push fail silently? If silent-fail, the mechanics-validation implication is significant.

### Overall Grade: B
Third process-clean week that met most mechanics-validation criteria but with two visible regressions: (1) **push cadence 13/15 = 86.7%** snaps the Weeks 12-13 perfect 30/30 two-week streak — Mon 7/27 + Wed 7/29 midday commits missing, with Wed midday particularly costly on FOMC day; (2) **SPY-relative print flips to -1.30% red** after two weekly beats (Wks 12-13 +38/+118 bps), phase gap to SPY RE-EXPANDS from -1.89% to ~-3.19%. Zero rule violations across five sessions with hawkish FOMC + Core PCE + four mega-cap AMC prints; XLK re-entry Day 10-14 gate-blocked all 5-of-5 cleanly; XLP GTC Path-C renewal executed cleanly Fri despite Path-B wrapper limitation surfacing as a hard block (Sat 8/1 weekend GTC-expiry risk ELIMINATED, mission-critical Fri task DELIVERED); two HWM ratchets fired same day Tue (first same-day 2-ratchet of Phase 1, delivered on Mon plan); XLI held +3.41% Fri close despite Wed's -3.28% biggest-Phase-1-drawdown. Capped at B (not A- or B+) because (a) push cadence regression is real and includes the highest-stakes single-session of the quarter (Wed FOMC), (b) XLP Path-B blocked by wrapper limitation is a mechanics gap that DELIVERED a documented Path-C fallback but leaves the underlying wrapper needing enhancement — Path-C cost ~$830 of ratchet-basis stop-protection, (c) bars-endpoint SPY switch DEFERRED SIXTH consecutive week ignoring Week 13's "MUST resolve Week 14 Mon" escalation, (d) fourteenth straight week below the 75-85% deployment target with XLK re-entry now unfired SIX consecutive weeks and 33 no-trade sessions in last 34, (e) XLB priority-watch RE-ESCALATED to $50.00 pre-cut proximity Fri close (0.83% cushion), (f) funding mismatch unreconciled two weeks past 90-day milestone. P&L (-130 bps vs SPY) not the basis for the grade — the defensive-cyclical mix behaved exactly as designed under risk-on rotation (preserves in risk-off, gives back in risk-on). Strategy edge in dispersion-rich rotation regime was Wks 12-13 finding; Wk 14 delivered the counter-regime and the mix underperformed cleanly — pattern-consistent with a defensive-cyclical strategy calibrated for slightly-below-median vol. 14 weeks remains far below the calibration horizon and the phase verdict comes at 3+ years, not 14 weeks.

### Key Lessons
- **The XLK re-entry 6-gate has now blocked 19 consecutive gate-tracked days (Wks 9-15) — the calibration itself is at trigger point for re-look**. Wk 14 review pre-committed "if two more full-clear-blocked days Mon/Tue = escalate gate-recalibration discussion"; Wk 15 Mon+Tue delivered exactly that. The gate as-configured requires (a) VIX <17 AND (b) XLK breakout print > $185 fresh AND (c) NQ risk-on AND (d) no binary event that day AND (e) breadth confirmation AND (f) 4-hour tape confirmation. In practice this excludes: any day with VIX 17+, any AMD/PLTR/META/AAPL/AMZN/MSFT earnings day (that's ~4-6 days per quarter), any FOMC-adjacent week, any NFP-adjacent day. That's ~40% of trading days pre-excluded. **The mechanics-validation question for Wk 16 is: is this gate as-designed or overtuned?** Not a rules-change discussion (2-week+ pattern threshold not yet met), but a documented tracking-question for the calibration reminder cycle.
- **RSP-relative print +1.53% vs +1.66% = -0.13% is the STRUCTURAL benchmark read** — the defensive-cyclical mix has fit vs equal-weight-S&P (essentially matches for the week), not vs cap-weighted SPY under mega-cap-tech re-leadership. ### Key Lessons
- **XLB GTC renewal executed via Path-C 6% + same-day HWM auto-ratchet fires = the cleanest Path-C renewal execution of Phase-1** — order placed AM with new $49.0868 stop, then mid-day HWM breach $52.22 → $52.67 auto-ratcheted stop UP to $49.5098 within hours.
